FILE: src/app/admin/actions.ts
============================================================

"use server";

import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { getCMSUser } from "@/lib/admin/auth";

export type LoginState = {
  error: string | null;
};

export async function login(
  _previousState: LoginState,
  formData: FormData
): Promise<LoginState> {
  const email = String(formData.get("email") ?? "").trim();
  const password = String(formData.get("password") ?? "");

  if (!email || !password) {
    return { error: "Please enter your email and password." };
  }

  const supabase = await createClient();

  const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
    email,
    password,
  });

  if (error) {
    return { error: "Invalid email or password." };
  }

  const cmsUser = await getCMSUser();

  if (!cmsUser) {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();

    return {
      error: "Your account is not authorized to access the CMS.",
    };
  }

  redirect("/admin");
}

export async function logout() {
  const supabase = await createClient();

  await supabase.auth.signOut();

  redirect("/admin/login");
}

  title: "Admin Login | Sangbe Foundation",

============================================================
FILE: src/app/api/contact/route.ts
============================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";

import { sendStaffNotification } from "@/lib/email/notifications";
import {
  getClientIp,
  rateLimit,
  rateLimitResponse,
} from "@/lib/security/rate-limit";
import { isHoneypotTriggered } from "@/lib/security/spam";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

type ContactPayload = {
  name?: unknown;
  email?: unknown;
  phone?: unknown;
  subject?: unknown;
  message?: unknown;
  website?: unknown;
};

function cleanString(value: unknown) {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function isValidEmail(email: string) {
  return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email);
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const ip = getClientIp(request);

  const limit = rateLimit({
    key: `contact:${ip}`,
    limit: 5,
    windowMs: 10 * 60 * 1000,
  });

  if (!limit.success) {
    return rateLimitResponse(limit.resetAt);
  }

  try {
    const body = (await request.json()) as ContactPayload;

    if (isHoneypotTriggered(body.website)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: true,
          message: "Your message has been received.",
        },
        { status: 201 }
      );
    }

    const name = cleanString(body.name);
    const email = cleanString(body.email).toLowerCase();
    const phone = cleanString(body.phone);
    const subject = cleanString(body.subject);
    const message = cleanString(body.message);

    if (!name) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Please provide your name." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!email || !isValidEmail(email)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please provide a valid email address.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!message) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please enter your message.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (name.length > 150) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Name is too long." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (email.length > 254) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Email address is too long." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (phone.length > 50) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Phone number is too long." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (subject.length > 200) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Subject is too long." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (message.length > 5000) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Message is too long." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("contact_submissions")
      .insert({
        name,
        email,
        phone: phone || null,
        subject: subject || null,
        message,
        status: "new",
      })
      .select("id, created_at")
      .single();

    if (error) {
      console.error("Contact submission database error:", {
        code: error.code,
        message: error.message,
      });

      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error:
            "We could not submit your message at this time. Please try again.",
        },
        { status: 500 }
      );
    }

    const notification = await sendStaffNotification({
      subject: `New contact message from ${name}`,
      title: "New Contact Message",
      fields: [
        { label: "Reference", value: data.id },
        { label: "Name", value: name },
        { label: "Email", value: email },
        { label: "Phone", value: phone || "Not provided" },
        { label: "Subject", value: subject || "Not provided" },
        { label: "Message", value: message },
        {
          label: "Received",
          value: new Date(data.created_at).toLocaleString(),
        },
      ],
    });

    if (!notification.success) {
      console.warn(
        `Contact submission ${data.id} was stored, but staff email notification was not sent.`
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: true,
        message: "Your message has been received.",
        reference: data.id,
      },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Contact API error:", error);

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: false,
        error:
          "We could not submit your message at this time. Please try again.",
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: src/app/api/donations/route.ts
============================================================
import { randomUUID } from "crypto";
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";

import { sendStaffNotification } from "@/lib/email/notifications";
import {
  getClientIp,
  rateLimit,
  rateLimitResponse,
} from "@/lib/security/rate-limit";
import { isHoneypotTriggered } from "@/lib/security/spam";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

type DonationMethod =
  | "wave"
  | "bank"
  | "international_bank";

type DonationPayload = {
  method?: unknown;
  amount?: unknown;
  donorName?: unknown;
  donorEmail?: unknown;
  donorPhone?: unknown;
  anonymous?: unknown;
  website?: unknown;
};

const VALID_METHODS = new Set<DonationMethod>([
  "wave",
  "bank",
  "international_bank",
]);

function cleanString(value: unknown) {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function isValidEmail(email: string) {
  return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email);
}

function createDonationReference() {
  const date = new Date()
    .toISOString()
    .slice(0, 10)
    .replaceAll("-", "");

  const suffix = randomUUID()
    .replaceAll("-", "")
    .slice(0, 8)
    .toUpperCase();

  return `SANGBE-${date}-${suffix}`;
}

function getPaymentMethodLabel(method: DonationMethod) {
  switch (method) {
    case "wave":
      return "Wave";
    case "bank":
      return "Ecobank Gambia";
    case "international_bank":
      return "International Bank Transfer";
  }
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const ip = getClientIp(request);

  const limit = rateLimit({
    key: `donations:${ip}`,
    limit: 5,
    windowMs: 10 * 60 * 1000,
  });

  if (!limit.success) {
    return rateLimitResponse(limit.resetAt);
  }

  try {
    const body = (await request.json()) as DonationPayload;

    if (isHoneypotTriggered(body.website)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: true,
          message: "Your donation request has been created.",
        },
        { status: 201 }
      );
    }

    const method = cleanString(body.method) as DonationMethod;
    const donorName = cleanString(body.donorName);
    const donorEmail = cleanString(body.donorEmail).toLowerCase();
    const donorPhone = cleanString(body.donorPhone);
    const anonymous = body.anonymous === true;

    const amount =
      typeof body.amount === "number"
        ? body.amount
        : Number(body.amount);

    if (!VALID_METHODS.has(method)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please select a valid payment method.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount < 1) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please enter a valid donation amount.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (amount > 10_000_000) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "The donation amount exceeds the supported limit.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const roundedAmount = Math.round(amount * 100) / 100;

    if (Math.abs(amount - roundedAmount) > Number.EPSILON) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error:
            "Donation amounts may contain no more than two decimal places.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (donorEmail && !isValidEmail(donorEmail)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please provide a valid email address.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (
      donorName.length > 150 ||
      donorEmail.length > 254 ||
      donorPhone.length > 50
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "One or more donor fields exceed the allowed length.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const reference = createDonationReference();

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("donation_intents")
      .insert({
        reference,
        donor_name:
          anonymous || !donorName ? null : donorName,
        donor_email:
          anonymous || !donorEmail ? null : donorEmail,
        donor_phone:
          anonymous || !donorPhone ? null : donorPhone,
        anonymous,
        method,
        amount: roundedAmount,
        currency: "GMD",
        donor_reference: null,
        payment_status: "pending",
      })
      .select(
        `
          id,
          reference,
          amount,
          currency,
          method,
          payment_status,
          created_at
        `
      )
      .single();

    if (error) {
      console.error("Donation database error:", {
        code: error.code,
        message: error.message,
      });

      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error:
            "We could not create your donation request. Please try again.",
        },
        { status: 500 }
      );
    }

    const paymentMethodLabel =
      getPaymentMethodLabel(method);

    const notification = await sendStaffNotification({
      subject: `New pending donation — GMD ${roundedAmount.toLocaleString()} — ${paymentMethodLabel}`,
      title: "New Pending Donation",
      fields: [
        {
          label: "Donation Reference",
          value: data.reference,
        },
        {
          label: "Amount",
          value: `GMD ${Number(data.amount).toLocaleString()}`,
        },
        {
          label: "Payment Method",
          value: paymentMethodLabel,
        },
        {
          label: "Payment Status",
          value: "PENDING — NOT YET VERIFIED",
        },
        {
          label: "Anonymous",
          value: anonymous ? "Yes" : "No",
        },
        {
          label: "Donor Name",
          value: anonymous
            ? "Anonymous"
            : donorName || "Not provided",
        },
        {
          label: "Donor Email",
          value: anonymous
            ? "Not retained"
            : donorEmail || "Not provided",
        },
        {
          label: "Donor Phone",
          value: anonymous
            ? "Not retained"
            : donorPhone || "Not provided",
        },
        {
          label: "Created",
          value: new Date(data.created_at).toLocaleString(),
        },
      ],
    });

    if (!notification.success) {
      console.warn(
        `Donation intent ${data.reference} was stored, but staff email notification was not sent.`
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: true,
        message: "Your donation request has been created.",
        donation: {
          reference: data.reference,
          amount: Number(data.amount),
          currency: data.currency,
          method: data.method,
          status: data.payment_status,
        },
      },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Donation API error:", error);

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: false,
        error:
          "We could not create your donation request. Please try again.",
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: src/app/api/request-help/route.ts
============================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";

import { sendStaffNotification } from "@/lib/email/notifications";
import {
  getClientIp,
  rateLimit,
  rateLimitResponse,
} from "@/lib/security/rate-limit";
import { isHoneypotTriggered } from "@/lib/security/spam";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

type RequestHelpPayload = {
  name?: unknown;
  phone?: unknown;
  email?: unknown;
  location?: unknown;
  beneficiary?: unknown;
  assistanceType?: unknown;
  urgency?: unknown;
  details?: unknown;
  consent?: unknown;
  website?: unknown;
};

function cleanString(value: unknown) {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function isValidEmail(email: string) {
  return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email);
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const ip = getClientIp(request);

  const limit = rateLimit({
    key: `request-help:${ip}`,
    limit: 3,
    windowMs: 10 * 60 * 1000,
  });

  if (!limit.success) {
    return rateLimitResponse(limit.resetAt);
  }

  try {
    const body = (await request.json()) as RequestHelpPayload;

    if (isHoneypotTriggered(body.website)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: true,
          message: "Your assistance request has been received.",
        },
        { status: 201 }
      );
    }

    const name = cleanString(body.name);
    const phone = cleanString(body.phone);
    const email = cleanString(body.email).toLowerCase();
    const location = cleanString(body.location);
    const beneficiary = cleanString(body.beneficiary);
    const assistanceType = cleanString(body.assistanceType);
    const urgency = cleanString(body.urgency);
    const details = cleanString(body.details);
    const consent = body.consent === true;

    if (!name) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Please provide your name." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!phone) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Please provide a phone number." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (email && !isValidEmail(email)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please provide a valid email address.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!location) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Please provide your location." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!beneficiary) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please indicate who needs assistance.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!assistanceType) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please select the type of assistance needed.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!urgency) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please indicate the urgency of the request.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!details) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please provide details about the assistance needed.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!consent) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error:
            "Please confirm that the information may be reviewed by Sangbe Foundation.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (name.length > 150) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Name is too long." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (phone.length > 50) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Phone number is too long." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (email.length > 254) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Email address is too long." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (location.length > 250) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Location is too long." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (beneficiary.length > 200) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Beneficiary information is too long.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (assistanceType.length > 150) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Assistance type is too long.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (urgency.length > 100) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Urgency value is too long." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (details.length > 5000) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Request details are too long.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("assistance_requests")
      .insert({
        name,
        phone,
        email: email || null,
        location,
        beneficiary,
        assistance_type: assistanceType,
        urgency,
        details,
        consent,
        status: "new",
      })
      .select("id, created_at")
      .single();

    if (error) {
      console.error("Assistance request database error:", {
        code: error.code,
        message: error.message,
      });

      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error:
            "We could not submit your assistance request at this time. Please try again.",
        },
        { status: 500 }
      );
    }

    const notification = await sendStaffNotification({
      subject: `New assistance request — ${urgency}`,
      title: "New Assistance Request",
      fields: [
        { label: "Reference", value: data.id },
        { label: "Name", value: name },
        { label: "Phone", value: phone },
        { label: "Email", value: email || "Not provided" },
        { label: "Location", value: location },
        { label: "Beneficiary", value: beneficiary },
        { label: "Assistance Type", value: assistanceType },
        { label: "Urgency", value: urgency },
        { label: "Details", value: details },
        {
          label: "Received",
          value: new Date(data.created_at).toLocaleString(),
        },
      ],
    });

    if (!notification.success) {
      console.warn(
        `Assistance request ${data.id} was stored, but staff email notification was not sent.`
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: true,
        message: "Your assistance request has been received.",
        reference: data.id,
      },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Request Help API error:", error);

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: false,
        error:
          "We could not submit your assistance request at this time. Please try again.",
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: src/app/api/volunteer/route.ts
============================================================
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";

import { sendStaffNotification } from "@/lib/email/notifications";
import {
  getClientIp,
  rateLimit,
  rateLimitResponse,
} from "@/lib/security/rate-limit";
import { isHoneypotTriggered } from "@/lib/security/spam";
import { supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase/admin";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

type VolunteerPayload = {
  name?: unknown;
  email?: unknown;
  phone?: unknown;
  location?: unknown;
  occupation?: unknown;
  interests?: unknown;
  availability?: unknown;
  experience?: unknown;
  motivation?: unknown;
  consent?: unknown;
  website?: unknown;
};

function cleanString(value: unknown) {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function isValidEmail(email: string) {
  return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email);
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  const ip = getClientIp(request);

  const limit = rateLimit({
    key: `volunteer:${ip}`,
    limit: 3,
    windowMs: 10 * 60 * 1000,
  });

  if (!limit.success) {
    return rateLimitResponse(limit.resetAt);
  }

  try {
    const body = (await request.json()) as VolunteerPayload;

    if (isHoneypotTriggered(body.website)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: true,
          message: "Your volunteer application has been received.",
        },
        { status: 201 }
      );
    }

    const name = cleanString(body.name);
    const email = cleanString(body.email).toLowerCase();
    const phone = cleanString(body.phone);
    const location = cleanString(body.location);
    const occupation = cleanString(body.occupation);
    const availability = cleanString(body.availability);
    const experience = cleanString(body.experience);
    const motivation = cleanString(body.motivation);
    const consent = body.consent === true;

    const interests = Array.isArray(body.interests)
      ? body.interests
          .filter(
            (interest): interest is string =>
              typeof interest === "string"
          )
          .map((interest) => interest.trim())
          .filter(Boolean)
      : [];

    if (!name) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, error: "Please provide your name." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!email || !isValidEmail(email)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please provide a valid email address.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!phone) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please provide your phone number.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!location) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please provide your location.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (interests.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please select at least one area of interest.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!availability) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please indicate your availability.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!motivation) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Please tell us why you would like to volunteer.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!consent) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error:
            "Please confirm that Sangbe Foundation may review your application.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (
      name.length > 150 ||
      email.length > 254 ||
      phone.length > 50 ||
      location.length > 250 ||
      occupation.length > 200 ||
      availability.length > 200 ||
      experience.length > 3000 ||
      motivation.length > 3000
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error:
            "One or more fields exceed the allowed length.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (
      interests.length > 20 ||
      interests.some((interest) => interest.length > 100)
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error: "Invalid volunteer interests.",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const uniqueInterests = [...new Set(interests)];

    const { data, error } = await supabaseAdmin
      .from("volunteer_applications")
      .insert({
        name,
        email,
        phone,
        location,
        occupation: occupation || null,
        interests: uniqueInterests,
        availability,
        experience: experience || null,
        motivation,
        consent,
        status: "new",
      })
      .select("id, created_at")
      .single();

    if (error) {
      console.error("Volunteer application database error:", {
        code: error.code,
        message: error.message,
      });

      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          success: false,
          error:
            "We could not submit your volunteer application at this time. Please try again.",
        },
        { status: 500 }
      );
    }

    const notification = await sendStaffNotification({
      subject: `New volunteer application — ${name}`,
      title: "New Volunteer Application",
      fields: [
        { label: "Reference", value: data.id },
        { label: "Name", value: name },
        { label: "Email", value: email },
        { label: "Phone", value: phone },
        { label: "Location", value: location },
        { label: "Occupation", value: occupation || "Not provided" },
        { label: "Interests", value: uniqueInterests.join(", ") },
        { label: "Availability", value: availability },
        { label: "Experience", value: experience || "Not provided" },
        { label: "Motivation", value: motivation },
        {
          label: "Received",
          value: new Date(data.created_at).toLocaleString(),
        },
      ],
    });

    if (!notification.success) {
      console.warn(
        `Volunteer application ${data.id} was stored, but staff email notification was not sent.`
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: true,
        message: "Your volunteer application has been received.",
        reference: data.id,
      },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Volunteer API error:", error);

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: false,
        error:
          "We could not submit your volunteer application at this time. Please try again.",
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  Home,
  RefreshCw,
} from "lucide-react";

export default function ErrorPage({
  reset,
}: {
  error: Error & { digest?: string };
  reset: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <main className="relative flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-[#073b29] px-6 py-20">
      {/* Background decoration */}
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute -right-40 -top-40 h-[520px] w-[520px] rounded-full border border-white/[0.06]" />
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-48 -left-40 h-[560px] w-[560px] rounded-full border border-white/[0.05]" />

      <div className="relative z-10 mx-auto max-w-2xl text-center">
        <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.07]">
          <AlertTriangle
            size={28}
            strokeWidth={1.7}
            className="text-[#72dda0]"
          />
        </div>

        <p className="mt-8 text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.28em] text-[#72dda0]">
          Something went wrong
        </p>

        <h1 className="mt-5 text-5xl font-bold tracking-[-0.045em] text-white sm:text-6xl">
          We hit an unexpected problem.
        </h1>

        <p className="mx-auto mt-6 max-w-xl text-base leading-8 text-white/65 sm:text-lg">
          We couldn&apos;t complete your request. You can try again or
          return to the homepage.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-9 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 sm:flex-row">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => reset()}
            className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#72dda0] px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#073b29] transition hover:bg-[#8be7ad] sm:w-auto"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={17} />
            Try Again
          </button>

          <Link
            href="/"
            className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-white/[0.06] px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-white/[0.1] sm:w-auto"
          >
            <Home size={17} />
            Return Home
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-14 border-t border-white/10 pt-7">
          <p className="text-sm text-white/45">
            If the problem continues, please contact Sangbe Foundation.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

}

FILE: src/app/events/[slug]/page.tsx
============================================================

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";

import {
  ArrowLeft,
  CalendarDays,
  Clock,
  MapPin,
  Images,
} from "lucide-react";

import Navbar from "@/components/layout/Navbar";
import Footer from "@/components/layout/Footer";
import EventGallery from "@/components/events/EventGallery";

import {
  events,
  formatEventDate,
  getEventBySlug,
} from "@/data/events";

type EventPageProps = {
  params: Promise<{
    slug: string;
  }>;
};

export function generateStaticParams() {
  return events.map((event) => ({
    slug: event.slug,
  }));
}

export async function generateMetadata({
  params,
}: EventPageProps): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const event = getEventBySlug(slug);

  if (!event) {
    return {
      title: "Event Not Found | Sangbe Foundation",
    };
  }

  return {
    title: `${event.title} | Sangbe Foundation`,
    description: event.excerpt,
  };
}

export default async function EventDetailPage({
  params,
}: EventPageProps) {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const event = getEventBySlug(slug);

  if (!event) {
    notFound();
  }

  const gallery = event.gallery ?? [];

  return (
    <>
      <Navbar />

      <main>
        {/* Event hero */}
        <section className="relative overflow-hidden bg-[#073b29]">
          {event.image && (
            <>
              <Image
                src={event.image}
                alt={event.title}
                fill
                priority
                className="object-cover"
                sizes="100vw"
              />

              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[#052f20]/70" />
            </>
          )}

          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#042f20]/95 via-[#073b29]/75 to-[#073b29]/35" />

          <div className="relative z-10 mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 pb-20 pt-36 sm:pt-40 lg:px-8 lg:pb-24 lg:pt-44">
            <Link
              href="/events"
              className="mb-8 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-white/70 transition hover:text-white"
            >
              <ArrowLeft size={16} />
              Back to Events
            </Link>

            <span className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-[#72dda0]">
              {event.category}
            </span>

            <h1 className="mt-5 max-w-4xl text-4xl font-bold leading-[1.08] tracking-[-0.04em] text-white sm:text-5xl lg:text-[68px] lg:leading-[1.03]">
              {event.title}
            </h1>

            <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap gap-x-7 gap-y-4 text-sm text-white/80">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <CalendarDays size={18} className="text-[#72dda0]" />
                {event.endDate
                  ? `${formatEventDate(event.date)} – ${formatEventDate(event.endDate)}`
                  : formatEventDate(event.date)}
              </div>

              {event.time && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Clock size={18} className="text-[#72dda0]" />
                  {event.time}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <MapPin size={18} className="text-[#72dda0]" />
                {event.location}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Event content */}
        <section className="bg-white py-16 lg:py-24">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-6 lg:px-8">
            <p className="text-lg font-medium leading-8 text-[#34443a] sm:text-xl sm:leading-9">
              {event.excerpt}
            </p>

            <div className="mt-10 space-y-6">
              {event.description.map((paragraph, index) => (
                <p
                  key={index}
                  className="text-base leading-8 text-[#68766e] sm:text-lg"
                >
                  {paragraph}
                </p>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Event photo gallery */}
        {gallery.length > 0 && (
          <section className="bg-[#f4f8f5] py-16 lg:py-24">
            <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 lg:px-8">
              <div className="mb-10 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-5">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[#178f4c]">
                    <Images size={17} />
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em]">
                      Photo Gallery
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <h2 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-bold tracking-[-0.03em] text-[#142019] sm:text-4xl">
                    Moments from the Visit
                  </h2>

                  <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-7 text-[#68766e] sm:text-base">
                    Explore photographs documenting the Foundation's
                    activities and engagements.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <span className="text-sm font-medium text-[#68766e]">
                  {gallery.length} photographs
                </span>
              </div>

              <EventGallery images={gallery} title={event.title} />
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {/* Back navigation */}
        <section className="bg-white py-10">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-6 lg:px-8">
            <div className="border-t border-[#e1e8e3] pt-8">
              <Link
                href="/events"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-3 font-semibold text-[#178f4c] transition hover:text-[#0d6834]"
              >
                <ArrowLeft size={18} />
                View All Events
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </>
  );
}

"use client";

import {
  AlertTriangle,
  RefreshCw,
} from "lucide-react";

export default function GlobalError({
  reset,
}: {
  error: Error & { digest?: string };
  reset: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <body className="m-0">
        <main className="relative flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-[#073b29] px-6 py-20">
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute -right-40 -top-40 h-[520px] w-[520px] rounded-full border border-white/[0.06]" />
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute -bottom-48 -left-40 h-[560px] w-[560px] rounded-full border border-white/[0.05]" />

          <div className="relative z-10 mx-auto max-w-2xl text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.07]">
              <AlertTriangle
                size={28}
                strokeWidth={1.7}
                className="text-[#72dda0]"
              />
            </div>

            <p className="mt-8 text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.28em] text-[#72dda0]">
              Sangbe Foundation
            </p>

            <h1 className="mt-5 text-5xl font-bold tracking-[-0.045em] text-white sm:text-6xl">
              Something went wrong.
            </h1>

            <p className="mx-auto mt-6 max-w-xl text-base leading-8 text-white/65 sm:text-lg">
              An unexpected error prevented the website from loading
              correctly. Please try again.
            </p>

            <div className="mt-9 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 sm:flex-row">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => reset()}
                className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#72dda0] px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#073b29] transition hover:bg-[#8be7ad] sm:w-auto"
              >
                <RefreshCw size={17} />
                Try Again
              </button>

              <a
                href="/"
                className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-white/15 bg-white/[0.06] px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-white/[0.1] sm:w-auto"
              >
                Return Home
              </a>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-14 border-t border-white/10 pt-7">
              <p className="text-sm text-white/45">
                Stronger Communities. Brighter Futures.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

}

  box-sizing: border-box;
}

html {
  scroll-behavior: smooth;
}

  margin: 0;

}

============================================================
FILE: src/app/team/[slug]/page.tsx
============================================================
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";

import Navbar from "@/components/layout/Navbar";
import Footer from "@/components/layout/Footer";

import TeamProfileHero from "@/components/team/profile/TeamProfileHero";
import TeamProfileContent from "@/components/team/profile/TeamProfileContent";
import TeamProfileCTA from "@/components/team/profile/TeamProfileCTA";

import {
  getTeamMember,
  teamMembers,
} from "@/data/team";

type PageProps = {
  params: Promise<{
    slug: string;
  }>;
};

export function generateStaticParams() {
  return teamMembers.map((member) => ({
    slug: member.slug,
  }));
}

export async function generateMetadata({
  params,
}: PageProps): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { slug } = await params;

  const member = getTeamMember(slug);

  if (!member) {
    return {
      title: "Team Member | Sangbe Foundation",
    };
  }

  return {
    title: `${member.name} | Sangbe Foundation`,
    description:
      member.shortBio ||
      `Learn more about ${member.name}, ${member.role} at Sangbe Foundation.`,
  };
}

export default async function TeamMemberPage({
  params,
}: PageProps) {
  const { slug } = await params;

  const member = getTeamMember(slug);

  if (!member) {
    notFound();
  }

  return (
    <>
      <Navbar />

      <main>
        <TeamProfileHero member={member} />

        <TeamProfileContent member={member} />

        <TeamProfileCTA />
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </>
  );
}

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowRight, UserRound } from "lucide-react";

import Reveal from "@/components/ui/Reveal";
import {
  Stagger,
  StaggerItem,
} from "@/components/ui/Stagger";

import { teamMembers } from "@/data/team";

/* -------------------------------------------------------
   Featured team members
------------------------------------------------------- */

const featuredSlugs = [
  "mohammed-abdallah-sangbe",
  "catherine-andrews",
  "henry-ofosu-kyeremeh",
  "alimatou-sankareh",
];

const team = featuredSlugs
  .map((slug) =>
    teamMembers.find((member) => member.slug === slug)
  )
  .filter(
    (member): member is (typeof teamMembers)[number] =>
      Boolean(member)
  );

/* -------------------------------------------------------
   Team Preview
------------------------------------------------------- */

export default function TeamPreview() {
  return (
    <section className="bg-[#f5f8f6] py-16 sm:py-20 lg:py-28">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-5 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <Reveal>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-7 lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="mb-4 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-[#178f4c] sm:mb-5 sm:tracking-[0.25em]">
                Our People
              </p>

              <h2 className="max-w-2xl text-[2.35rem] font-bold leading-[1.04] tracking-[-0.035em] text-[#142019] sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl lg:leading-[1.08]">
                The people behind{" "}
                <span className="text-[#19944f]">
                  Sangbe Foundation.
                </span>
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-5 max-w-2xl text-base leading-7 text-[#68766e] sm:mt-6 sm:text-lg sm:leading-8">
                Our team brings together people committed to serving
                communities, building partnerships, and advancing the
                Foundation&apos;s mission.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Desktop link */}
            <Link

              Meet Our Team

              <ArrowRight

              />
            </Link>
          </div>
        </Reveal>

        <Stagger className="mt-10 grid gap-5 sm:mt-12 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:mt-14 lg:grid-cols-4">
          {team.map((member) => (
            <StaggerItem
              key={member.slug}
              className="h-full"
            >
              <Link
                href={`/team/${member.slug}`}
                className="block h-full"
                aria-label={`View ${member.name}'s profile`}
              >
                <article className="group flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-[24px] border border-[#dde7e0] bg-white transition duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-[0_18px_45px_rgba(20,50,35,0.07)]">
                  {/* Photo */}

                        src={member.image}

                        sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, (max-width: 1024px) 50vw, 25vw"
                      />

                        <div className="absolute -bottom-20 -left-16 h-52 w-52 rounded-full bg-[#edf5ef]" />

FILE: src/components/admin/AdminLoginForm.tsx

"use client";

import { useActionState } from "react";
import { LockKeyhole, Mail, LoaderCircle } from "lucide-react";

import { login } from "@/app/admin/actions";

export default function AdminLoginForm() {
  const [state, action, pending] = useActionState(login, {
    error: null,
  });

  return (
    <form action={action} className="mt-8 space-y-5">
      <div>
        <label
          htmlFor="email"
          className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-[#34443a]"
        >
          Email address
        </label>

        <div className="relative">
          <Mail
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#829087]"
          />

          <input
            id="email"
            name="email"
            type="email"
            required
            autoComplete="username"
            placeholder="Enter your email"
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-[#dce6df] bg-white py-3.5 pl-12 pr-4 text-sm text-[#142019] outline-none transition focus:border-[#178f4c] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#178f4c]/10"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label
          htmlFor="password"
          className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-[#34443a]"
        >
          Password
        </label>

        <div className="relative">
          <LockKeyhole
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#829087]"
          />

          <input
            id="password"
            name="password"
            type="password"
            required
            autoComplete="current-password"
            placeholder="Enter your password"
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-[#dce6df] bg-white py-3.5 pl-12 pr-4 text-sm text-[#142019] outline-none transition focus:border-[#178f4c] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#178f4c]/10"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {state.error && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-600">
          {state.error}
        </p>
      )}

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={pending}
        className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#178f4c] px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-[#10743c] disabled:opacity-60"
      >
        {pending && <LoaderCircle size={17} className="animate-spin" />}
        {pending ? "Signing in..." : "Sign In"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: src/components/contact/ContactSection.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import { FormEvent, useState } from "react";
import {
  CheckCircle2,
  Loader2,
  Mail,
  MapPin,
  MessageCircle,
  Phone,
  Send,
} from "lucide-react";
import HoneypotField from "@/components/ui/HoneypotField";

type FormData = {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  subject: string;
  message: string;
};

const initialForm: FormData = {
  name: "",
  email: "",
  phone: "",
  subject: "",
  message: "",
};

export default function ContactSection() {
  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormData>(initialForm);
  const [website, setWebsite] = useState("");

  const [status, setStatus] = useState<
    "idle" | "submitting" | "success" | "error"
  >("idle");

  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState("");

  function updateField(
    field: keyof FormData,
    value: string
  ) {
    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [field]: value,
    }));

    if (status === "error") {
      setStatus("idle");
      setErrorMessage("");
    }
  }

async function handleSubmit(
  event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>
) {
  event.preventDefault();

  setStatus("submitting");
  setErrorMessage("");

  try {
    const response = await fetch("/api/contact", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        ...form,
        website,
      }),
    });

    const data = await response.json().catch(() => null);

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        data?.error ||
          data?.message ||
          "We could not send your message. Please try again."
      );
    }

    if (!data?.success) {
      throw new Error(
        "We could not confirm your submission. Please try again."
      );
    }

    setStatus("success");
    setForm(initialForm);
    setWebsite("");
  } catch (error) {
    setStatus("error");

    setErrorMessage(
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Something went wrong. Please try again."
    );
  }
}

  return (
    <section className="bg-white py-24 lg:py-28">
      <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-7xl gap-16 px-6 lg:grid-cols-[0.75fr_1.25fr] lg:px-8">
        {/* Contact details */}
        <div>
          <p className="mb-5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-[#178f4c]">
            Get in Touch
          </p>

          <h2 className="max-w-lg text-4xl font-bold leading-[1.08] tracking-[-0.03em] text-[#142019] lg:text-5xl">
            Start a{" "}
            <span className="text-[#19944f]">conversation.</span>
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-6 max-w-md text-base leading-7 text-[#68766e]">
            Send us a message using the form and our team will review your
            enquiry and respond when possible.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-10 space-y-4">
            <div className="flex gap-4 rounded-[20px] border border-[#e0e8e3] bg-[#f8faf8] p-5">
              <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#eaf7ef]">
                <MapPin size={20} className="text-[#19944f]" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="font-bold text-[#142019]">
                  Location
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-[#68766e]">
                  Busumbala, West Coast Region
                  <br />
                  The Gambia
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-4 rounded-[20px] border border-[#e0e8e3] bg-[#f8faf8] p-5">
              <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#eaf7ef]">
                <Phone size={20} className="text-[#19944f]" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="font-bold text-[#142019]">
                  Phone / WhatsApp
                </p>

                <a
                  href="tel:+22087779250"
                  className="mt-1 block text-sm font-medium text-[#68766e] transition-colors hover:text-[#19944f]"
                >
                  +220 8777 79250
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>


           <div className="flex gap-4 rounded-[20px] border border-[#e0e8e3] bg-[#f8faf8] p-5">
              <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#eaf7ef]">
                <Mail size={20} className="text-[#19944f]" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="font-bold text-[#142019]">
                  Email
                </p>

                <a
                  href="mailto:sangbefoundation@gmail.com"
                  className="mt-1 block text-sm font-medium text-[#68766e] transition-colors hover:text-[#19944f]"
                >
                  sangbefoundation@gmail.com
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>
            </div>

          <div className="mt-7 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-[18px] bg-[#eaf7ef] p-5">
            <MessageCircle
              size={19}
              className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-[#19944f]"
            />

            <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-[#53665a]">
              Looking for assistance? Please use our dedicated{" "}
              <a
                href="/request-help"
                className="font-semibold text-[#178f4c] hover:underline"
              >
                Request Help
              </a>{" "}
              form so we can collect the appropriate information.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Form */}
        <div className="rounded-[30px] border border-[#dfe8e2] bg-[#f8faf8] p-7 md:p-10 lg:p-12">
          <div className="mb-9">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-[#178f4c]">
              Send a Message
            </p>

            <h3 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-bold tracking-[-0.025em] text-[#142019]">
              How can we help?
            </h3>
          </div>

          {status === "success" ? (
            <div className="flex min-h-[470px] flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
              <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#e3f6ea]">
                <CheckCircle2
                  size={30}
                  className="text-[#19944f]"
                />
              </div>

              <h3 className="mt-6 text-2xl font-bold text-[#142019]">
                Message received.
              </h3>

              <p className="mt-3 max-w-md text-base leading-7 text-[#68766e]">
                Thank you for contacting Sangbe Foundation. Your message has
                been submitted successfully.
              </p>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setStatus("idle")}
                className="mt-7 font-semibold text-[#178f4c] transition hover:text-[#126f3c]"
              >
                Send another message
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
              <HoneypotField
                value={website}
                onChange={setWebsite}
              />
              <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="name"
                    className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-[#25342b]"
                  >
                    Full Name *
                  </label>

                  <input
                    id="name"
                    type="text"
                    required
                    autoComplete="name"
                    value={form.name}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateField("name", event.target.value)
                    }
                    placeholder="Your name"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-white px-4 py-3.5 text-[#142019] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#a2ada6] focus:border-[#19944f] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="email"
                    className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-[#25342b]"
                  >
                    Email Address *
                  </label>

                  <input
                    id="email"
                    type="email"
                    required
                    autoComplete="email"
                    value={form.email}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateField("email", event.target.value)
                    }
                    placeholder="you@example.com"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-white px-4 py-3.5 text-[#142019] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#a2ada6] focus:border-[#19944f] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="phone"
                    className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-[#25342b]"
                  >
                    Phone Number
                  </label>

                  <input
                    id="phone"
                    type="tel"
                    autoComplete="tel"
                    value={form.phone}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateField("phone", event.target.value)
                    }
                    placeholder="Optional"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-white px-4 py-3.5 text-[#142019] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#a2ada6] focus:border-[#19944f] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="subject"
                    className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-[#25342b]"
                  >
                    Subject *
                  </label>

                  <input
                    id="subject"
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={form.subject}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateField("subject", event.target.value)
                    }
                    placeholder="What is this about?"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-white px-4 py-3.5 text-[#142019] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#a2ada6] focus:border-[#19944f] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-6">
                <label
                  htmlFor="message"
                  className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-[#25342b]"
                >
                  Message *
                </label>

                <textarea
                  id="message"
                  required
                  rows={7}
                  value={form.message}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateField("message", event.target.value)
                  }
                  placeholder="Write your message here..."
                  className="w-full resize-none rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-white px-4 py-3.5 text-[#142019] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#a2ada6] focus:border-[#19944f] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10"
                />
              </div>

              {status === "error" && (
                <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
                  {errorMessage}
                </div>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={status === "submitting"}
                className="group mt-7 inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-[#19944f] px-7 py-4 font-semibold text-white transition-all duration-300 hover:bg-[#168545] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60 sm:w-auto"
              >
                {status === "submitting" ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2
                      size={18}
                      className="animate-spin"
                    />
                    Sending...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    Send Message

                    <Send
                      size={17}
                      className="transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:translate-x-1"
                    />
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </form>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: src/components/donate/DonationForm.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  Building2,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Copy,
  Globe2,
  Heart,
  Loader2,
  Smartphone,
} from "lucide-react";

import HoneypotField from "@/components/ui/HoneypotField";

type DonationMethod =
  | "wave"
  | "bank"
  | "international_bank";

type DonationResult = {
  reference: string;
  amount: number;
  currency: string;
  method: DonationMethod;
  status: string;
};

const PRESET_AMOUNTS = [50, 100, 200, 500];

const paymentMethods = [
  {
    id: "wave" as const,
    name: "Wave",
    description: "Local mobile money donation",
    icon: Smartphone,
  },
  {
    id: "bank" as const,
    name: "Bank Transfer",
    description: "Ecobank Gambia",
    icon: Building2,
  },
  {
    id: "international_bank" as const,
    name: "International Transfer",
    description: "International bank transfer",
    icon: Globe2,
  },
];

export default function DonationForm() {
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);

  const [method, setMethod] =
    useState<DonationMethod | null>(null);

  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");

  const [donorName, setDonorName] = useState("");
  const [donorEmail, setDonorEmail] = useState("");
  const [donorPhone, setDonorPhone] = useState("");

  const [anonymous, setAnonymous] =
    useState(false);

  // Invisible anti-spam field.
  // Normal users leave this empty.
  const [website, setWebsite] = useState("");

  const [donation, setDonation] =
    useState<DonationResult | null>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(false);

  const [error, setError] =
    useState("");

  const numericAmount = Number(amount);

  function choosePreset(value: number) {
    setAmount(String(value));
    setError("");
  }

  function selectMethod(value: DonationMethod) {
    setMethod(value);
    setError("");
  }

  function continueToAmount() {
    if (!method) {
      setError(
        "Please select a payment method."
      );
      return;
    }

    setError("");
    setStep(2);
  }

  async function createDonation() {
    if (
      !Number.isFinite(numericAmount) ||
      numericAmount < 1
    ) {
      setError(
        "Please enter a valid donation amount."
      );
      return;
    }

    if (!method) {
      setError(
        "Please select a payment method."
      );
      setStep(1);
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        "/api/donations",
        {
          method: "POST",

          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
          },

          body: JSON.stringify({
            method,
            amount: numericAmount,
            donorName,
            donorEmail,
            donorPhone,
            anonymous,
            website,
          }),
        }
      );

      const data = await response
        .json()
        .catch(() => null);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          data?.error ||
            "We could not create your donation request."
        );
      }

      if (
        !data?.success ||
        !data?.donation?.reference
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          "We could not confirm your donation request."
        );
      }

      setDonation(data.donation);
      setStep(3);
    } catch (error) {
      setError(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Something went wrong. Please try again."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <section
      id="donate"
      className="bg-[#f5f8f6] py-20 lg:py-28"
    >
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-6 lg:px-8">
        {/* Heading */}

        <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl text-center">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-[#178f4c]">
            Make a Donation
          </p>

          <h2 className="mt-4 text-4xl font-bold tracking-[-0.035em] text-[#142019] sm:text-5xl">
            Support our work.
          </h2>

          <p className="mt-5 text-base leading-7 text-[#68766e]">
            Choose your preferred payment
            method and donation amount. We
            will provide the appropriate
            payment details.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Step indicator */}

        <div className="mx-auto mt-12 max-w-3xl">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3">
            <StepIndicator
              number={1}
              label="Select Method"
              active={step === 1}
              complete={step > 1}
            />

            <StepIndicator
              number={2}
              label="Enter Amount"
              active={step === 2}
              complete={step > 2}
            />

            <StepIndicator
              number={3}
              label="Get Details"
              active={step === 3}
              complete={false}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Main card */}

        <div className="mx-auto mt-10 max-w-3xl overflow-hidden rounded-[28px] border border-[#dce6df] bg-white shadow-[0_20px_60px_rgba(20,55,35,0.07)]">
          <HoneypotField
            value={website}
            onChange={setWebsite}
          />

          {/* STEP 1 */}

          {step === 1 && (
            <div className="p-7 sm:p-10">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#178f4c]">
                  Step 1
                </p>

                <h3 className="mt-3 text-2xl font-bold text-[#142019]">
                  Choose a payment method
                </h3>

                <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-[#68766e]">
                  Select how you would like
                  to make your donation.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-8 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
                {paymentMethods.map(
                  (paymentMethod) => {
                    const Icon =
                      paymentMethod.icon;

                    const selected =
                      method ===
                      paymentMethod.id;

                    return (
                      <button
                        key={
                          paymentMethod.id
                        }
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                          selectMethod(
                            paymentMethod.id
                          )
                        }
                        className={`relative rounded-[20px] border p-5 text-left transition-all ${
                          selected
                            ? "border-[#19944f] bg-[#eff9f2] ring-2 ring-[#19944f]/10"
                            : "border-[#dfe7e2] bg-white hover:border-[#bcd8c5] hover:bg-[#fafcfb]"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {selected && (
                          <div className="absolute right-4 top-4 flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#19944f]">
                            <Check
                              size={14}
                              className="text-white"
                            />
                          </div>
                        )}

                        <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#e8f5ec]">
                          <Icon
                            size={21}
                            className="text-[#19944f]"
                          />
                        </div>

                        <p className="mt-5 font-bold text-[#142019]">
                          {
                            paymentMethod.name
                          }
                        </p>

                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[#7c8981]">
                          {
                            paymentMethod.description
                          }
                        </p>
                      </button>
                    );
                  }
                )}
              </div>

              {error && (
                <ErrorMessage
                  message={error}
                />
              )}

              <div className="mt-8 flex justify-end">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={
                    continueToAmount
                  }
                  className="group inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#19944f] px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-[#168545]"
                >
                  Continue

                  <ArrowRight
                    size={17}
                    className="transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1"
                  />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* STEP 2 */}

          {step === 2 && (
            <div className="p-7 sm:p-10">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#178f4c]">
                Step 2
              </p>

              <h3 className="mt-3 text-2xl font-bold text-[#142019]">
                Enter your donation
              </h3>

              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-[#68766e]">
                Every contribution helps
                Sangbe Foundation support
                communities and people in
                need.
              </p>

              {/* Amount */}

              <div className="mt-8">
                <label
                  htmlFor="amount"
                  className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-[#25342b]"
                >
                  Donation Amount (GMD)
                </label>

                <div className="relative">
                  <span className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 font-bold text-[#19944f]">
                    D
                  </span>

                  <input
                    id="amount"
                    type="number"
                    min="1"
                    step="0.01"
                    value={amount}
                    onChange={(event) => {
                      setAmount(
                        event.target.value
                      );
                      setError("");
                    }}
                    placeholder="Enter amount"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-white py-4 pl-10 pr-4 text-lg font-semibold text-[#142019] outline-none transition focus:border-[#19944f] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
                  {PRESET_AMOUNTS.map(
                    (value) => (
                      <button
                        key={value}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                          choosePreset(
                            value
                          )
                        }
                        className={`rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold transition ${
                          amount ===
                          String(value)
                            ? "border-[#19944f] bg-[#eaf7ef] text-[#178f4c]"
                            : "border-[#dce5df] text-[#53645a] hover:border-[#a9cfb5]"
                        }`}
                      >
                        GMD {value}
                      </button>
                    )
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Optional donor info */}

              <div className="mt-9 border-t border-[#e7ede9] pt-8">
                <h4 className="font-bold text-[#142019]">
                  Donor information
                </h4>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#7a8880]">
                  Optional. This can help
                  the Foundation identify
                  and acknowledge your
                  donation.
                </p>

                <label className="mt-5 flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-3">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={anonymous}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setAnonymous(
                        event.target.checked
                      )
                    }
                    className="h-4 w-4 accent-[#19944f]"
                  />

                  <span className="text-sm font-medium text-[#4f6156]">
                    Make this donation
                    anonymously
                  </span>
                </label>

                {!anonymous && (
                  <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={donorName}
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        setDonorName(
                          event.target.value
                        )
                      }
                      placeholder="Full name"
                      autoComplete="name"
                      className="rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-white px-4 py-3.5 text-sm text-[#142019] outline-none transition focus:border-[#19944f] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10"
                    />

                    <input
                      type="email"
                      value={donorEmail}
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        setDonorEmail(
                          event.target.value
                        )
                      }
                      placeholder="Email address"
                      autoComplete="email"
                      className="rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-white px-4 py-3.5 text-sm text-[#142019] outline-none transition focus:border-[#19944f] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10"
                    />

                    <input
                      type="tel"
                      value={donorPhone}
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        setDonorPhone(
                          event.target.value
                        )
                      }
                      placeholder="Phone number"
                      autoComplete="tel"
                      className="rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-white px-4 py-3.5 text-sm text-[#142019] outline-none transition focus:border-[#19944f] focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10 sm:col-span-2"
                    />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {error && (
                <ErrorMessage
                  message={error}
                />
              )}

              <div className="mt-9 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setError("");
                    setStep(1);
                  }}
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-[#dbe5de] px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-[#53645a] transition hover:bg-[#f6f9f7]"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft size={17} />
                  Back
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={loading}
                  onClick={
                    createDonation
                  }
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-[#19944f] px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-[#168545] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
                >
                  {loading ? (
                    <>
                      <Loader2
                        size={17}
                        className="animate-spin"
                      />
                      Preparing...
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      Get Payment Details
                      <ArrowRight
                        size={17}
                      />
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* STEP 3 */}

          {step === 3 &&
            donation && (
              <PaymentDetails
                donation={donation}
              />
            )}
        </div>

        {/* Registration */}

        <div className="mx-auto mt-7 flex max-w-3xl items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center text-xs text-[#7b8980]">
          <CheckCircle2
            size={15}
            className="text-[#19944f]"
          />

          <span>
            Sangbe Foundation • Charity
            Registration No.{" "}
            <strong className="text-[#53645a]">
              2019/C8931
            </strong>
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function StepIndicator({
  number,
  label,
  active,
  complete,
}: {
  number: number;
  label: string;
  active: boolean;
  complete: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="relative flex flex-col items-center text-center">
      <div
        className={`relative z-10 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-sm font-bold ${
          active || complete
            ? "bg-[#19944f] text-white"
            : "border border-[#ccd9d0] bg-white text-[#849188]"
        }`}
      >
        {complete ? (
          <Check size={16} />
        ) : (
          number
        )}
      </div>

      <p
        className={`mt-2 text-xs font-semibold ${
          active || complete
            ? "text-[#178f4c]"
            : "text-[#89958d]"
        }`}
      >
        {label}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

}) {

        Complete your payment
      </h3>

        Use the payment details below
        to complete your donation. Your
        donation remains pending until
        payment has been confirmed.
      </p>

      {/* Summary */}

      <div className="mt-8 grid gap-3 rounded-[20px] bg-[#f3f8f5] p-5 sm:grid-cols-2">

            donation.amount
          ).toLocaleString()}`}
        />

        />

      <div className="mt-7">
        {donation.method ===

            }
          />
        )}

        {donation.method ===

            }

        )}

          <code className="break-all text-base font-bold text-[#073b29]">
            {donation.reference}
          </code>

          <CopyButton
            value={
              donation.reference
            }
          />
        </div>

        <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[#748178]">
          Where your payment provider
          allows a reference or
          description, use this reference
          so the Foundation can identify
          your donation.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-7 rounded-[18px] border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-5">
        <p className="text-sm font-bold text-amber-900">
          Payment confirmation
        </p>

        <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-amber-800">
          Creating this donation request
          does not confirm that payment
          has been received. The
          Foundation will verify the
          payment separately.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
