function PaymentCard({
  title,
  subtitle,
  children,
}: {
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-[22px] border border-[#dce6df]">
      <div className="bg-[#073b29] px-5 py-4">
        <p className="font-bold text-white">
          {title}
        </p>

        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-white/60">
          {subtitle}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="divide-y divide-[#e7ede9] px-5">
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function DetailRow({
  label,
  value,
  copy = false,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  copy?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex gap-4 py-4">
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[#89958e]">
          {label}
        </p>

        <p className="mt-1 break-words text-sm font-semibold text-[#142019]">
          {value}
        </p>
      </div>

      {copy && (
        <CopyButton value={value} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryItem({
  label,
  value,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[#839087]">
        {label}
      </p>

      <p className="mt-1 font-bold text-[#142019]">
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function CopyButton({
  value,
}: {
  value: string;
}) {
  const [copied, setCopied] =
    useState(false);

  async function copyValue() {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(
        value
      );

      setCopied(true);

      window.setTimeout(
        () => setCopied(false),
        1500
      );
    } catch {
      setCopied(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={copyValue}
      className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 self-start rounded-lg border border-[#d7e4db] bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-[#53665a] transition hover:border-[#a9cfb5] hover:text-[#178f4c]"
    >
      {copied ? (
        <>
          <Check size={14} />
          Copied
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <Copy size={14} />
          Copy
        </>
      )}
    </button>
  );
}

function ErrorMessage({
  message,
}: {
  message: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
      {message}
    </div>
  );
}

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";

import {
  ArrowRight,
  CalendarDays,
  MapPin,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  Event,
  formatEventDate,
} from "@/data/events";

type EventCardProps = {
  event: Event;
};

export default function EventCard({ event }: EventCardProps) {
  return (
    <Link
      href={`/events/${event.slug}`}
      className="group flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-[26px] border border-[#dfe8e2] bg-white transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-[#bdd8c7] hover:shadow-[0_18px_45px_rgba(20,50,35,0.08)]"
    >
      <div className="relative h-[250px] overflow-hidden bg-[#0b4a34]">
        {event.image ? (
          <>
            <Image
              src={event.image}
              alt={event.title}
              fill
              className="object-cover transition-transform duration-700 group-hover:scale-[1.04]"
              sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 33vw"
            />

            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#052f20]/45 via-transparent to-transparent" />
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-[#0b5138] to-[#073b29]">
            <CalendarDays
              size={54}
              strokeWidth={1.4}
              className="text-white/25"
            />

            <div className="absolute -right-20 -top-20 h-64 w-64 rounded-full border border-white/10" />

            <div className="absolute -bottom-24 -left-16 h-56 w-56 rounded-full border border-white/[0.07]" />
          </div>
        )}

        <span className="absolute left-5 top-5 rounded-full bg-white/95 px-4 py-2 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-[#178f4c]">
          {event.category}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col p-7">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-[#19944f]">
          <CalendarDays size={16} />
          {formatEventDate(event.date)}
        </div>

        <h3 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-bold leading-tight tracking-[-0.02em] text-[#142019]">
          {event.title}
        </h3>

        <p className="mt-4 text-sm leading-7 text-[#68766e]">
          {event.excerpt}
        </p>

        <div className="mt-auto flex items-end justify-between gap-4 pt-8">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-[#8b9891]">
            <MapPin size={16} />
            <span>{event.location}</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#eaf7ef] text-[#19944f] transition-all duration-300 group-hover:bg-[#19944f] group-hover:text-white">
            <ArrowRight size={17} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/events/EventGallery.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Image from "next/image";
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, X } from "lucide-react";

type EventGalleryProps = {
  images: string[];
  title: string;
};

export default function EventGallery({
  images,
  title,
}: EventGalleryProps) {
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeIndex === null) return;

    function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setActiveIndex(null);
      }

      if (event.key === "ArrowRight") {
        setActiveIndex((current) =>
          current === null ? null : (current + 1) % images.length
        );
      }

      if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") {
        setActiveIndex((current) =>
          current === null
            ? null
            : (current - 1 + images.length) % images.length
        );
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
  }, [activeIndex, images.length]);

  if (!images.length) return null;

  const previous = () =>
    setActiveIndex((current) =>
      current === null ? null : (current - 1 + images.length) % images.length
    );

  const next = () =>
    setActiveIndex((current) =>
      current === null ? null : (current + 1) % images.length
    );

  return (
    <>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:gap-5 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {images.map((src, index) => (
          <button
            key={`${src}-${index}`}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveIndex(index)}
            aria-label={`View photo ${index + 1} of ${images.length}`}
            className="group relative aspect-[4/3] overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-[#eaf1ec] text-left"
          >
            <Image
              src={src}
              alt={`${title} — photograph ${index + 1}`}
              fill
              sizes="(max-width: 640px) 50vw, (max-width: 1024px) 33vw, 380px"
              className="object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105"
            />

            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/0 transition-colors group-hover:bg-black/10" />
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {activeIndex !== null && (
        <div
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          aria-label={`${title} photo viewer`}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-black/95 p-4"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveIndex(null)}
            aria-label="Close photo viewer"
            className="absolute right-4 top-4 z-20 rounded-full bg-white/15 p-3 text-white hover:bg-white/25"
          >
            <X size={22} />
          </button>

          <div className="relative h-[75vh] w-full max-w-6xl">
            <Image
              src={images[activeIndex]}
              alt={`${title} — photograph ${activeIndex + 1}`}
              fill
              sizes="100vw"
              className="object-contain"
            />
          </div>

          {images.length > 1 && (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={previous}
                aria-label="Previous photograph"
                className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 z-20 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-white/15 p-3 text-white hover:bg-white/25 sm:left-6"
              >
                <ChevronLeft size={25} />
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={next}
                aria-label="Next photograph"
                className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 z-20 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-white/15 p-3 text-white hover:bg-white/25 sm:right-6"
              >
                <ChevronRight size={25} />
              </button>
            </>
          )}

          <p className="absolute bottom-5 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 text-sm text-white/75">
            {activeIndex + 1} / {images.length}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

import EventCard from "./EventCard";

              Past Events
            </p>

                community.

              community initiatives.

          </div>
        </Reveal>

        <Stagger className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">

              <EventCard event={event} />
            </StaggerItem>
          ))}
        </Stagger>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

============================================================
FILE: src/components/gallery/GalleryGrid.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import {
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Images,
  MapPin,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import {
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";

import {
  galleryImages,
  GalleryCategory,
  GalleryImage,
} from "@/data/gallery";

const categories: Array<"All" | GalleryCategory> = [
  "All",
  "Awareness",
  "Community",
  "Disaster Relief",
  "Events",
];

export default function GalleryGrid() {
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] =
    useState<"All" | GalleryCategory>("All");

  const [selectedImage, setSelectedImage] =
    useState<GalleryImage | null>(null);

  const filteredImages = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeCategory === "All") {
      return galleryImages;
    }

    return galleryImages.filter(
      (image) => image.category === activeCategory
    );
  }, [activeCategory]);

  const selectedIndex = selectedImage
    ? filteredImages.findIndex(
        (image) => image.id === selectedImage.id
      )
    : -1;

  function showPrevious() {
    if (!selectedImage || filteredImages.length <= 1) return;

    const previousIndex =
      selectedIndex <= 0
        ? filteredImages.length - 1
        : selectedIndex - 1;

    setSelectedImage(filteredImages[previousIndex]);
  }

  function showNext() {
    if (!selectedImage || filteredImages.length <= 1) return;

    const nextIndex =
      selectedIndex >= filteredImages.length - 1
        ? 0
        : selectedIndex + 1;

    setSelectedImage(filteredImages[nextIndex]);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedImage) {
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
      return;
    }

    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";

    function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setSelectedImage(null);
      }

      if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") {
        showPrevious();
      }

      if (event.key === "ArrowRight") {
        showNext();
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);

    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [selectedImage, selectedIndex]);

  return (
    <>
      <section className="bg-white py-24 lg:py-28">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 lg:px-8">
          {/* Heading */}
          <div className="mb-12 grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:items-end">
            <div>
              <p className="mb-5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-[#178f4c]">
                Photos & Moments
              </p>

              <h2 className="max-w-xl text-4xl font-bold leading-[1.08] tracking-[-0.03em] text-[#142019] lg:text-5xl">
                See our work{" "}
                <span className="text-[#19944f]">
                  in action.
                </span>
              </h2>
            </div>

            <p className="max-w-lg text-lg leading-8 text-[#68766e] lg:justify-self-end">
              Explore photographs from Sangbe Foundation activities,
              engagements, and community initiatives.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Filters */}
          <div className="mb-12 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            {categories.map((category) => {
              const active = activeCategory === category;

              return (
                <button
                  key={category}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setActiveCategory(category);
                    setSelectedImage(null);
                  }}
                  className={`rounded-full px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition-all duration-300 ${
                    active
                      ? "bg-[#19944f] text-white shadow-[0_8px_20px_rgba(25,148,79,0.18)]"
                      : "border border-[#dce6df] bg-white text-[#637269] hover:border-[#a9cfb7] hover:bg-[#f5faf7] hover:text-[#178f4c]"
                  }`}
                >
                  {category}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {/* Gallery */}
          {filteredImages.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="grid auto-rows-[230px] grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {filteredImages.map((image, index) => {
                const featured = index % 5 === 0;

                return (
                  <button
                    key={image.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSelectedImage(image)}
                    className={`group relative overflow-hidden rounded-[26px] bg-[#eaf1ec] text-left ${
                      featured
                        ? "sm:row-span-2 lg:row-span-2"
                        : ""
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Image
                      src={image.src}
                      alt={image.alt}
                      fill
                      className="object-cover transition-transform duration-700 ease-out group-hover:scale-[1.04]"
                      sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, (max-width: 1024px) 50vw, 33vw"
                    />

                    <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#052f20]/80 via-[#052f20]/5 to-transparent opacity-80 transition-opacity duration-300 group-hover:opacity-100" />

                    <div className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 translate-y-2 p-6 transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:translate-y-0">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#72dda0]">
                        {image.category}
                      </span>

                      <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-white">
                        {image.title}
                      </h3>

                      {image.location && (
                        <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-white/65">
                          <MapPin size={14} />
                          {image.location}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="rounded-[28px] border border-[#dfe8e2] bg-[#f7faf8] px-8 py-16 text-center md:px-12">
              <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-[#eaf7ef]">
                <Images
                  size={28}
                  className="text-[#19944f]"
                />
              </div>

              <h3 className="mt-6 text-2xl font-bold text-[#142019]">
                Gallery photographs coming soon.
              </h3>

              <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-lg text-base leading-7 text-[#68766e]">
                Photographs from Sangbe Foundation activities and community
                engagements will be added here.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Lightbox */}
      {selectedImage && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-[#031d14]/95 px-4 py-6 backdrop-blur-sm"
          onClick={() => setSelectedImage(null)}
        >
          {/* Close */}
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Close image"
            onClick={() => setSelectedImage(null)}
            className="absolute right-5 top-5 z-20 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/10 text-white transition hover:bg-white/20"
          >
            <X size={23} />
          </button>

          {/* Previous */}
          {filteredImages.length > 1 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              aria-label="Previous image"
              onClick={(event) => {
                event.stopPropagation();
                showPrevious();
              }}
              className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 z-20 flex h-12 w-12 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/10 text-white transition hover:bg-white/20 md:left-8"
            >
              <ChevronLeft size={25} />
            </button>
          )}

          {/* Image container */}
          <div
            className="relative flex max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-6xl flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-[26px] bg-[#082c20]"
            onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <div className="relative min-h-[60vh] flex-1">
              <Image
                src={selectedImage.src}
                alt={selectedImage.alt}
                fill
                priority
                className="object-contain"
                sizes="90vw"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="border-t border-white/10 bg-[#073b29] px-6 py-5 md:px-8">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#72dda0]">
                    {selectedImage.category}
                  </p>

                  <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-white md:text-2xl">
                    {selectedImage.title}
                  </h3>
                </div>

                {(selectedImage.location ||
                  selectedImage.date) && (
                  <div className="text-sm text-white/55">
                    {selectedImage.location && (
                      <span>{selectedImage.location}</span>
                    )}

                    {selectedImage.location &&
                      selectedImage.date && (
                        <span className="mx-2">•</span>
                      )}

                    {selectedImage.date && (
                      <span>{selectedImage.date}</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Next */}
          {filteredImages.length > 1 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              aria-label="Next image"
              onClick={(event) => {
                event.stopPropagation();
                showNext();
              }}
              className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 z-20 flex h-12 w-12 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/10 text-white transition hover:bg-white/20 md:right-8"
            >
              <ChevronRight size={25} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import Image from "next/image";
import { ArrowUpRight, CalendarDays, MapPin } from "lucide-react";

import EventGallery from "@/components/events/EventGallery";
import { getEventBySlug } from "@/data/events";

export default function GhanaGalleryFeature() {
  const event = getEventBySlug(
    "ghana-humanitarian-cultural-visit-2026"
  );

  if (!event || !event.gallery?.length) {
    return null;
  }

  const featuredImages = event.gallery.slice(0, 12);

  return (
    <section className="bg-white py-16 sm:py-20 lg:py-24">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="grid items-center gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr] lg:gap-12">
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-[#178f4c]">
              Featured Collection
            </span>

            <h2 className="mt-4 max-w-xl text-3xl font-bold tracking-[-0.035em] text-[#142019] sm:text-4xl lg:text-[44px]">
              Sangbe Foundation in Ghana
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-5 max-w-xl text-base leading-8 text-[#68766e]">
              A look back at Mrs. Catherine Andrews&apos;
              humanitarian and cultural visit to Ghana in September
              2026, representing Sangbe Foundation through
              international outreach, cultural engagement, and
              community solidarity.
            </p>

            <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-3 text-sm text-[#68766e]">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                <CalendarDays size={17} className="text-[#178f4c]" />
                September 2026
              </span>

              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
                <MapPin size={17} className="text-[#178f4c]" />
                Ghana
              </span>
            </div>

            <Link
              href={`/events/${event.slug}`}
              className="mt-8 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-[#178f4c] px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[#10743c]"
            >
              Read the Full Story
              <ArrowUpRight size={17} />
            </Link>
          </div>

          {event.image && (
            <Link
              href={`/events/${event.slug}`}
              className="group relative block aspect-[4/3] overflow-hidden rounded-[26px] bg-[#eaf1ec]"
            >
              <Image
                src={event.image}
                alt="Sangbe Foundation Ghana outreach visit"
                fill
                sizes="(max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 50vw"
                className="object-cover transition-transform duration-500 group-hover:scale-105"
              />

              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#073b29]/55 via-transparent to-transparent" />

              <div className="absolute bottom-5 left-5 right-5 flex items-center justify-between text-white">
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold">
                  Ghana Outreach 2026
                </span>

                <span className="rounded-full bg-white/20 px-3 py-1 text-xs backdrop-blur-md">
                  {event.gallery.length} Photos
                </span>
              </div>
            </Link>
          )}
        </div>

          <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">

              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#142019] sm:text-2xl">

/* -------------------------------------------------------
   Desktop Stat Card
------------------------------------------------------- */

function StatCard({
  icon,
  value,
  label,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  value: string;
  label: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#063b27]/85 p-5 text-white backdrop-blur-sm">
      <div className="mb-6 text-[#6be09e]">
        {icon}
      </div>

      <p className="break-words text-sm font-bold xl:text-base">
        {value}
      </p>

      <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-white/60">
        {label}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------
   Mobile Stat Card
------------------------------------------------------- */

function MobileStatCard({
  icon,
  value,
  label,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  value: string;
  label: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-[18px] bg-[#073b29] px-5 py-4 text-white">
      <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/10 text-[#6be09e]">
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div className="min-w-0">
        <p className="font-bold leading-5">
          {value}
        </p>

        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-white/60">
          {label}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

                    action.primary
                      ? "bg-white/15 text-white"
                      : "bg-[#eaf7ef] text-[#178a49]"
                  }`}
                >
                  <Icon size={26} />
                </div>

                <h3 className="mt-8 text-2xl font-bold tracking-[-0.025em]">
                  {action.title}
                </h3>

                <p
                  className={`mt-4 leading-7 ${
                    action.primary ? "text-white/75" : "text-[#66736b]"
                  }`}
                >
                  {action.description}
                </p>

                <div className="mt-auto pt-8">
                  <Link
                    href={action.href}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-bold transition hover:gap-3 ${
                      action.primary ? "text-white" : "text-[#178a49]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {action.label}
                    <ArrowRight size={18} />
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";

import {
  ArrowUpRight,
  CalendarDays,
  Images,
  MapPin,
} from "lucide-react";

import { getEventBySlug } from "@/data/events";

export default function GhanaVisitHighlight() {
  const event = getEventBySlug(
    "ghana-humanitarian-cultural-visit-2026"
  );

  if (!event) return null;

  return (
    <section className="overflow-hidden bg-[#f4f8f5] py-16 sm:py-20 lg:py-24">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="grid items-center gap-10 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:gap-16">
          {/* Photograph */}
          <div className="relative">
            <div className="relative aspect-[4/4.3] overflow-hidden rounded-[28px] bg-[#e1eee5] sm:aspect-[5/4] lg:aspect-[4/4.3]">
              {event.image ? (
                <Image

                  alt="Sangbe Foundation's international outreach visit to Ghana"

FILE: src/components/layout/Navbar.tsx

"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Menu, X } from "lucide-react";

import { navigation } from "@/data/navigation";

export default function Navbar() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <header className="absolute left-0 top-0 z-50 w-full">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="mt-5 flex h-[84px] items-center justify-between rounded-2xl bg-white/95 px-5 shadow-sm backdrop-blur-md lg:px-8">
          {/* Official Sangbe Foundation Logo */}
          <Link
              href="/"
              onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
              className="flex h-full w-[180px] shrink-0 items-center sm:w-[200px]"
              aria-label="Sangbe Foundation home"
            >
              <Image
                src="/images/sangbe-logov2.png"
                alt="Sangbe Foundation"
                width={1600}
                height={900}
                priority
                className="max-h-[68px] w-full object-contain object-left"
              />
            </Link>

          {/* Desktop Navigation */}
          <nav className="hidden items-center gap-7 lg:flex">
            {navigation.map((item) => (
              <Link
                key={item.name}
                href={item.href}
                className="text-sm font-medium text-[#35433b] transition hover:text-[#178a49]"
              >
                {item.name}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </nav>

          {/* Desktop Donate */}
          <div className="hidden lg:block">
            <Link
              href="/donate"
              className="rounded-lg bg-[#178a49] px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[#0f6937]"
            >
              Donate
            </Link>
          </div>

          {/* Mobile Menu Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setOpen((current) => !current)}
            className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-[#e2e9e5] text-[#17201b] transition hover:bg-[#f5f8f6] lg:hidden"
            aria-label={open ? "Close menu" : "Open menu"}
            aria-expanded={open}
          >
            {open ? (
              <X size={22} />
            ) : (
              <Menu size={22} />
            )}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Mobile Navigation */}
        {open && (
          <div className="mt-2 rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-xl lg:hidden">
            <nav className="flex flex-col">
              {navigation.map((item) => (
                <Link
                  key={item.name}
                  href={item.href}
                  onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                  className="border-b border-[#edf1ee] px-2 py-4 font-medium text-[#35433b] transition hover:text-[#178a49] last:border-none"
                >
                  {item.name}
                </Link>
              ))}

              <Link
                href="/donate"
                onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                className="mt-4 rounded-lg bg-[#178a49] px-5 py-3.5 text-center font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[#0f6937]"
              >
                Donate Now
              </Link>
            </nav>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

import {
  Ear,
  Search,
  HandHeart,
  Users,
} from "lucide-react";

import Reveal from "@/components/ui/Reveal";
import {
  Stagger,
  StaggerItem,
} from "@/components/ui/Stagger";

const steps = [
  {
    number: "01",
    title: "Listen",
    description:
      "We begin by listening to people and understanding the challenges affecting their communities.",
    icon: Ear,
  },
  {
    number: "02",
    title: "Understand",
    description:
      "We consider the needs, circumstances, and type of support that can make a meaningful difference.",
    icon: Search,
  },
  {
    number: "03",
    title: "Respond",
    description:
      "We turn understanding into practical action through awareness, assistance, and community support.",
    icon: HandHeart,
  },
  {
    number: "04",
    title: "Build",
    description:
      "We work with communities toward stronger participation, greater awareness, and lasting positive change.",
    icon: Users,
  },
];

export default function HowWeWork() {
  return (
    <section className="bg-[#f5f8f6] py-24 lg:py-28">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 lg:px-8">
        <Reveal>
          <div className="mx-auto mb-14 max-w-2xl text-center">
            <p className="mb-5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-[#178f4c]">
              How We Work
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold leading-[1.08] tracking-[-0.03em] text-[#142019] lg:text-5xl">
              Community needs come{" "}
              <span className="text-[#19944f]">first.</span>
            </h2>

            <p className="mx-auto mt-6 max-w-xl text-base leading-7 text-[#68766e]">
              Our approach starts with understanding the people we serve and
              turning that understanding into practical action.
            </p>
          </div>
        </Reveal>

        <Stagger className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          {steps.map((step, index) => {
            const Icon = step.icon;

            return (
              <StaggerItem
                key={step.title}
                className="h-full"
              >
                <div className="group relative flex h-full min-h-[290px] flex-col rounded-[24px] border border-[#dfe8e2] bg-white p-7 transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-[#bdd8c7] hover:shadow-[0_14px_35px_rgba(20,50,35,0.07)]">
                  {/* Connector */}
                  {index < steps.length - 1 && (
                    <div className="absolute left-[calc(100%+1px)] top-[55px] hidden w-5 border-t border-dashed border-[#bcd8c6] lg:block" />
                  )}

                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                    <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-[#eaf7ef] transition-all duration-300 group-hover:bg-[#19944f]">
                      <Icon
                        size={24}
                        className="text-[#19944f] transition-colors duration-300 group-hover:text-white"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#b0bbb4]">
                      {step.number}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <h3 className="mt-7 text-xl font-bold text-[#142019]">
                    {step.title}

============================================================
FILE: src/components/request-help/RequestHelpForm.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import { FormEvent, useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Loader2,
  Send,
} from "lucide-react";

import HoneypotField from "@/components/ui/HoneypotField";

type RequestForm = {
  name: string;
  phone: string;
  email: string;
  location: string;
  beneficiary: string;
  assistanceType: string;
  urgency: string;
  details: string;
  consent: boolean;
};

const initialForm: RequestForm = {
  name: "",
  phone: "",
  email: "",
  location: "",
  beneficiary: "",
  assistanceType: "",
  urgency: "",
  details: "",
  consent: false,
};

export default function RequestHelpForm() {
  const [form, setForm] =
    useState<RequestForm>(initialForm);

  const [website, setWebsite] =
    useState("");

  const [status, setStatus] = useState<
    "idle" | "submitting" | "success" | "error"
  >("idle");

  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] =
    useState("");

  function updateField<K extends keyof RequestForm>(
    field: K,
    value: RequestForm[K]
  ) {
    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [field]: value,
    }));

    if (status === "error") {
      setStatus("idle");
      setErrorMessage("");
    }
  }

  async function handleSubmit(
    event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>
  ) {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (!form.consent) {
      setStatus("error");
      setErrorMessage(
        "Please confirm that the information provided is accurate."
      );
      return;
    }

    setStatus("submitting");
    setErrorMessage("");

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        "/api/request-help",
        {
          method: "POST",

          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
          },

          body: JSON.stringify({
            ...form,
            website,
          }),
        }
      );

      const data = await response
        .json()
        .catch(() => null);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          data?.error ||
            data?.message ||
            "We could not submit your request. Please try again."
        );
      }

      if (!data?.success) {
        throw new Error(
          "We could not confirm your submission. Please try again."
        );
      }

      setStatus("success");
      setForm(initialForm);
      setWebsite("");
    } catch (error) {
      setStatus("error");

      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Something went wrong. Please try again."
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <section
      id="request-form"
      className="scroll-mt-24 bg-[#f5f8f6] py-24 lg:py-28"
    >
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl">
          <div className="mb-12 text-center">
            <p className="mb-5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-[#178f4c]">
              Assistance Request
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold tracking-[-0.03em] text-[#142019] lg:text-5xl">
              Tell us how we may{" "}
              <span className="text-[#19944f]">
                help.
              </span>
            </h2>

            <p className="mx-auto mt-5 max-w-2xl text-base leading-7 text-[#68766e]">
              Please provide as much relevant
              information as possible. Fields
              marked with an asterisk are
              required.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-[30px] border border-[#dfe8e2] bg-white p-7 shadow-[0_15px_45px_rgba(20,50,35,0.04)] md:p-10 lg:p-12">
            {status === "success" ? (
              <div className="flex min-h-[450px] flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
                <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#e5f7eb]">
                  <CheckCircle2
                    size={31}
                    className="text-[#19944f]"
                  />
                </div>

                <h3 className="mt-6 text-3xl font-bold text-[#142019]">
                  Your request has been
                  submitted.
                </h3>

                <p className="mt-4 max-w-lg text-base leading-7 text-[#68766e]">
                  Thank you for providing the
                  information. Sangbe Foundation
                  will review the request and may
                  contact you if additional
                  information is needed.
                </p>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setStatus("idle")
                  }
                  className="mt-8 font-semibold text-[#178f4c] hover:underline"
                >
                  Submit another request
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
                <HoneypotField
                  value={website}
                  onChange={setWebsite}
                />

                <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
                  <Field
                    label="Full Name"
                    required
                    value={form.name}
                    onChange={(value) =>
                      updateField(
                        "name",
                        value
                      )
                    }
                    placeholder="Your full name"
                    autoComplete="name"
                  />

                  <Field
                    label="Phone / WhatsApp"
                    required
                    type="tel"
                    value={form.phone}
                    onChange={(value) =>
                      updateField(
                        "phone",
                        value
                      )
                    }
                    placeholder="+220..."
                    autoComplete="tel"
                  />

                  <Field
                    label="Email Address"
                    type="email"
                    value={form.email}
                    onChange={(value) =>
                      updateField(
                        "email",
                        value
                      )
                    }
                    placeholder="Optional"
                    autoComplete="email"
                  />

                  <Field
                    label="Location / Community"
                    required
                    value={form.location}
                    onChange={(value) =>
                      updateField(
                        "location",
                        value
                      )
                    }
                    placeholder="e.g. Busumbala"
                  />

                  <SelectField
                    label="Who needs assistance?"
                    required
                    value={form.beneficiary}
                    onChange={(value) =>
                      updateField(
                        "beneficiary",
                        value
                      )
                    }
                    options={[
                      [
                        "myself",
                        "Myself",
                      ],
                      [
                        "family",
                        "My family",
                      ],
                      [
                        "individual",
                        "Another individual",
                      ],
                      [
                        "community",
                        "My community",
                      ],
                      [
                        "other",
                        "Other",
                      ],
                    ]}
                  />

                  <SelectField
                    label="Type of Assistance"
                    required
                    value={
                      form.assistanceType
                    }
                    onChange={(value) =>
                      updateField(
                        "assistanceType",
                        value
                      )
                    }
                    options={[
                      [
                        "essential-support",
                        "Essential Support",
                      ],
                      [
                        "disaster-relief",
                        "Disaster / Emergency Relief",
                      ],
                      [
                        "community-support",
                        "Community Assistance",
                      ],
                      [
                        "drug-support",
                        "Drug Abuse Related Support",
                      ],
                      [
                        "other",
                        "Other",
                      ],
                    ]}
                  />

                  <div className="md:col-span-2">
                    <SelectField
                      label="How urgent is the situation?"
                      required
                      value={form.urgency}
                      onChange={(value) =>
                        updateField(
                          "urgency",
                          value
                        )
                      }
                      options={[
                        [
                          "standard",
                          "Standard",
                        ],
                        [
                          "important",
                          "Important",
                        ],
                        [
                          "urgent",
                          "Urgent",
                        ],
                      ]}
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="md:col-span-2">
                    <label
                      htmlFor="request-details"
                      className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-[#25342b]"
                    >
                      Tell us about the
                      situation{" "}
                      <span className="text-[#19944f]">
                        *
                      </span>
                    </label>

                    <textarea
                      id="request-details"
                      required
                      rows={7}
                      value={form.details}
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        updateField(
                          "details",
                          event.target.value
                        )
                      }
                      placeholder="Describe what has happened, what assistance is needed, and any other information that may help us understand the situation."
                      className="w-full resize-none rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-[#fbfcfb] px-4 py-3.5 text-[#142019] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#a2ada6] focus:border-[#19944f] focus:bg-white focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <label className="mt-7 flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-[18px] bg-[#f4f8f5] p-5">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={form.consent}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateField(
                        "consent",
                        event.target.checked
                      )
                    }
                    className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 accent-[#19944f]"
                  />

                  <span className="text-sm leading-6 text-[#59675f]">
                    I confirm that the
                    information provided is
                    accurate to the best of my
                    knowledge and understand
                    that submitting this request
                    does not guarantee
                    assistance.
                  </span>
                </label>

                <div className="mt-5 flex items-start gap-3 text-sm leading-6 text-[#7c8981]">
                  <AlertCircle
                    size={17}
                    className="mt-0.5 shrink-0"
                  />

                  <p>
                    This form is not an
                    emergency service. If there
                    is an immediate threat to
                    life or safety, seek
                    appropriate local emergency
                    assistance.
                  </p>
                </div>

                {status === "error" && (
                  <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
                    {errorMessage}
                  </div>
                )}

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={
                    status === "submitting"
                  }
                  className="group mt-8 inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-[#19944f] px-7 py-4 font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[#168545] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60 sm:w-auto"
                >
                  {status ===
                  "submitting" ? (
                    <>
                      <Loader2
                        size={18}
                        className="animate-spin"
                      />
                      Submitting...
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      Submit Request

                      <Send
                        size={17}
                        className="transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1"
                      />
                    </>
                  )}
                </button>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

type FieldProps = {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  placeholder?: string;
  type?: string;
  required?: boolean;
  autoComplete?: string;
};

function Field({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  placeholder,
  type = "text",
  required = false,
  autoComplete,
}: FieldProps) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-[#25342b]">
        {label}{" "}
        {required && (
          <span className="text-[#19944f]">
            *
          </span>
        )}
      </label>

      <input
        type={type}
        required={required}
        value={value}
        autoComplete={autoComplete}
        onChange={(event) =>
          onChange(event.target.value)
        }
        placeholder={placeholder}
        className="w-full rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-[#fbfcfb] px-4 py-3.5 text-[#142019] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#a2ada6] focus:border-[#19944f] focus:bg-white focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

type SelectFieldProps = {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  options: [string, string][];
  required?: boolean;
};

function SelectField({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  options,
  required = false,
}: SelectFieldProps) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-[#25342b]">
        {label}{" "}
        {required && (
          <span className="text-[#19944f]">
            *
          </span>
        )}
      </label>

      <select
        required={required}
        value={value}
        onChange={(event) =>
          onChange(event.target.value)
        }
        className="w-full rounded-xl border border-[#d9e3dc] bg-[#fbfcfb] px-4 py-3.5 text-[#142019] outline-none transition focus:border-[#19944f] focus:bg-white focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#19944f]/10"
      >
        <option value="">
          Select an option
        </option>

        {options.map(
          ([value, label]) => (
            <option
              key={value}
              value={value}
            >
              {label}
            </option>
          )
        )}
      </select>
    </div>
  );
}

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import {
  ArrowUpRight,
  UserRound,
} from "lucide-react";

import Reveal from "@/components/ui/Reveal";

import {
  teamMembers,
  type TeamMember,
} from "@/data/team";

/* -------------------------------------------------------
   Team groups
------------------------------------------------------- */

const leadership = teamMembers.filter(
  (member) => member.category === "leadership"
);

const team = teamMembers.filter(
  (member) => member.category !== "leadership"
);

/* -------------------------------------------------------
   Main Directory
------------------------------------------------------- */

export default function TeamDirectory() {
  return (
    <>
      <TeamSection
        eyebrow="Leadership"
        title="Foundation Leadership"
        description="Providing direction and helping guide the Foundation's mission, priorities, and continued development."
        members={leadership}
        featured
      />

      <TeamSection
        eyebrow="Our Team"
        title="The People Behind Sangbe Foundation"
        description="Meet the people contributing their experience, skills, and commitment to the Foundation's work across its different areas and chapters."
        members={team}
        muted
      />
    </>
  );
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------
   Team Section
------------------------------------------------------- */

function TeamSection({
  eyebrow,
  title,
  description,
  members,
  featured = false,
  muted = false,
}: {
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  members: TeamMember[];
  featured?: boolean;
  muted?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <section
      className={`py-20 lg:py-24 ${
        muted ? "bg-[#f4f8f5]" : "bg-white"
      }`}
    >
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 lg:px-8">
        {/* Section heading */}
        <Reveal>
          <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[0.8fr_1.2fr] lg:items-end">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-[#178f4c]">
                {eyebrow}
              </p>

              <h2 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-bold tracking-[-0.03em] text-[#142019] sm:text-4xl">
                {title}
              </h2>
            </div>

            <p className="max-w-2xl text-base leading-7 text-[#68766e] lg:justify-self-end">
              {description}
            </p>
          </div>
        </Reveal>

        {/* Team grid */}
        <div
          className={`mt-12 grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 ${
            featured
              ? "lg:max-w-[780px] lg:grid-cols-2"
              : "lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4"
          }`}
        >
          {members.map((member) => (
            <div
              key={member.slug}
              className="h-full"
            >
              <Link
                href={`/team/${member.slug}`}
                className="block h-full"
                aria-label={`View ${member.name}'s profile`}
              >
                <TeamCard
                  member={member}
                  featured={featured}
                />
              </Link>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------
   Team Member Card
------------------------------------------------------- */

function TeamCard({
  member,
  featured = false,
}: {
  member: TeamMember;
  featured?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <article className="group flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-[24px] border border-[#dde7e0] bg-white transition duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-[#c7ddce] hover:shadow-[0_18px_45px_rgba(20,50,35,0.08)]">
      {/* Member photo */}
      <div
        className={`relative overflow-hidden bg-[#e5efe8] ${
          featured
            ? "aspect-[4/4.15]"
            : "aspect-[4/4.35]"
        }`}
      >
        {member.image ? (
          <Image
            src={member.image}
            alt={member.name}
            fill
            className="object-cover object-top transition duration-500 group-hover:scale-[1.035]"
            sizes={
              featured
                ? "(max-width: 640px) 100vw, 390px"
                : "(max-width: 640px) 100vw, (max-width: 1024px) 50vw, 300px"
            }
          />
        ) : (
          <div className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center">
            {/* Decorative background */}
            <div className="absolute -right-20 -top-20 h-56 w-56 rounded-full bg-[#d6e9dc]" />

            <div className="absolute -bottom-24 -left-20 h-60 w-60 rounded-full bg-[#eef5f0]" />

            <div className="absolute left-8 top-8 h-16 w-16 rounded-full border border-[#cfe2d5]" />

            {/* Placeholder icon */}
            <div
              className={`relative flex items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-white/70 bg-white/65 shadow-sm backdrop-blur-sm ${
                featured
                  ? "h-28 w-28"
                  : "h-24 w-24"
              }`}
            >
              <UserRound
                size={featured ? 50 : 43}
                strokeWidth={1.3}
                className="text-[#789b85]"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Image overlay */}
        {member.image && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-[#073b29]/20 via-transparent to-transparent opacity-0 transition-opacity duration-300 group-hover:opacity-100" />
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Member information */}
      <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col p-6">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <h3
            className={`font-bold tracking-[-0.015em] text-[#142019] transition-colors duration-300 group-hover:text-[#178f4c] ${
              featured
                ? "text-xl"
                : "text-lg"
            }`}
          >
            {member.name}
          </h3>

          <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#19944f]">
            {member.role}
          </p>

          {member.chapter && (
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[#849087]">
              {member.chapter}
            </p>
          )}

          {member.shortBio && (
            <p className="mt-4 line-clamp-3 text-sm leading-6 text-[#68766e]">
              {member.shortBio}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Profile link */}
        <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-[#edf1ee] pt-4">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-[#7b8980] transition-colors duration-300 group-hover:text-[#178f4c]">
            View Profile
          </span>

          <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#eef7f1] transition duration-300 group-hover:bg-[#19944f]">
            <ArrowUpRight
              size={15}
              className="text-[#19944f] transition duration-300 group-hover:-translate-y-0.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 group-hover:text-white"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

  ArrowLeft,
  Users,

  return (
    <section className="bg-[#eef6f1] py-20">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-7 rounded-[28px] bg-[#073b29] p-8 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between lg:p-10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-5">
            <div className="hidden h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white/[0.09] sm:flex">
              <Users
                size={22}
                className="text-[#72dda0]"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-[#72dda0]">
                Sangbe Foundation
              </p>

              <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-white">
                Meet the rest of our team.
              </h2>
            </div>
          </div>

          <Link
            href="/team"
            className="group inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-[#073b29] transition hover:bg-[#f0f7f2]"
          >
            <ArrowLeft
              size={17}
              className="transition-transform group-hover:-translate-x-1"
            />

            Back to Our Team
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import {
  BriefcaseBusiness,
  UserRound,
} from "lucide-react";

import type { TeamMember } from "@/data/team";

export default function TeamProfileContent({
  member,
}: {
  member: TeamMember;
}) {
  const hasBio =
    member.bio &&
    member.bio.length > 0;

  const hasExpertise =
    member.expertise &&
    member.expertise.length > 0;

  return (
    <section className="bg-white py-24 lg:py-28">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="grid gap-14 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_320px]">
          {/* Main content */}
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-[#178f4c]">
              Profile
            </p>

            <h2 className="mt-4 text-4xl font-bold tracking-[-0.03em] text-[#142019]">
              About {member.name.split(" ")[0]}
            </h2>

            {hasBio ? (
              <div className="mt-7 max-w-3xl space-y-5">
                {member.bio?.map((paragraph, index) => (
                  <p
                    key={index}
                    className="text-base leading-8 text-[#68766e]"
                  >
                    {paragraph}
                  </p>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="mt-7 max-w-3xl rounded-[22px] border border-[#dfe8e2] bg-[#f7faf8] p-7">
                <div className="flex gap-4">
                  <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#e3f4e8]">
                    <UserRound
                      size={20}
                      className="text-[#19944f]"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-[#142019]">
                      Profile information coming soon
                    </h3>

                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-[#68766e]">
                      Additional information about {member.name}&apos;s
                      background and work with Sangbe Foundation will be
                      added here.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Sidebar */}
          <aside>
            <div className="rounded-[24px] border border-[#dfe8e2] bg-[#f7faf8] p-7">
              <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#e3f4e8]">
                <BriefcaseBusiness
                  size={20}
                  className="text-[#19944f]"
                />
              </div>

              <p className="mt-6 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#89958e]">
                Role
              </p>

              <p className="mt-2 font-bold text-[#142019]">
                {member.role}
              </p>

              {member.chapter && (
                <>
                  <div className="my-6 border-t border-[#dde6e0]" />

                  <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#89958e]">
                    Chapter
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#142019]">
                    {member.chapter}
                  </p>
                </>
              )}

              {hasExpertise && (
                <>
                  <div className="my-6 border-t border-[#dde6e0]" />

                  <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-[#89958e]">
                    Areas of Work
                  </p>

                  <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    {member.expertise?.map((item) => (
                      <span
                        key={item}
                        className="rounded-full border border-[#d5e7da] bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[#32714d]"
                      >
                        {item}
                      </span>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

type HoneypotFieldProps = {
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
};

export default function HoneypotField({
  value,
  onChange,
}: HoneypotFieldProps) {
  return (
    <div
      aria-hidden="true"
      className="absolute -left-[9999px] top-auto h-px w-px overflow-hidden"
    >
      <label htmlFor="website">
        Website
      </label>

      <input
        id="website"
        name="website"
        type="text"
        value={value}
        onChange={(event) =>
          onChange(event.target.value)
        }
        tabIndex={-1}
        autoComplete="off"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ui/Reveal.tsx

"use client";

import { motion } from "motion/react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

interface RevealProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  delay?: number;
  y?: number;
  className?: string;
}

export default function Reveal({
  children,
  delay = 0,
  y = 28,
  className = "",
}: RevealProps) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      className={className}
      initial={{
        opacity: 0,
        y,
      }}
      whileInView={{
        opacity: 1,
        y: 0,
      }}
      viewport={{
        once: true,
        amount: 0.15,
      }}
      transition={{
        duration: 0.65,
        delay,
        ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1],
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

FILE: src/components/ui/Stagger.tsx
============================================================
"use client";

import { motion } from "motion/react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

interface StaggerProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export function Stagger({
  children,
  className = "",
}: StaggerProps) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      className={className}
      initial="hidden"
      whileInView="visible"
      viewport={{
        once: true,
        amount: 0.12,
      }}
      variants={{
        hidden: {},
        visible: {
          transition: {
            staggerChildren: 0.1,
          },
        },
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

export function StaggerItem({
  children,
  className = "",
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      className={className}
      variants={{
        hidden: {
          opacity: 0,
          y: 24,
        },
        visible: {
          opacity: 1,
          y: 0,
          transition: {
            duration: 0.6,
            ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1],
          },
        },
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  );
}
